# Windows

Every project across all 39 lists that runs natively on Windows, or is reachable through WSL2 — **10,906** of 11,993 distinct repos, deduplicated to one row per repo.

Native first, then WSL2-only, each block by stars. A Homebrew-only project that ships a Windows build shows its releases page instead of a command that cannot run here.

[← back to the mega list](../README.md) · [7,588 inferred →](windows-inferred.md)

Page **4** of 5, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](windows.md) · [2](windows-2.md) · [3](windows-3.md) · **4** · [5](windows-5.md) — [continue on page 5 →](windows-5.md)

Snapshot 2026-11-28. Stars, language, licence and last-push come from the GitHub API and drift daily.

Filter the same data in the browser on the [Atlas site](https://aaa.jeremyfhall.com/catalog/). Spreadsheet edition: retired. The last copy, built 2026-10-22, stays available but is no longer updated. The website is the maintained edition. Its last workbooks: [dark](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/download/v2026.10.22/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-DARK.xlsx) · [light](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/download/v2026.10.22/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-LIGHT.xlsx).
